Implement the following plan:

# Cleanup: Revert Bot Over-Engineering & File Issue Report

## Context

The desloppify bot finds real issues (dead exports, missing tests, duplication), but the execution agent over-corrects — extracting single-use functions, creating satellite files, adding naming conventions, and stripping useful comments. Across 5 review cycles, the net effect has been: more files, more lines, more indirection, fewer comments. This violates the project's core principle: "every change should make the codebase smaller or more explicit."

This plan: (1) fixes the remaining code damage, (2) files a GitHub issue on `peteromallet/desloppify` documenting the patterns so the tool can be improved.

---

## Part 1: Code Cleanup (remaining over-engineering)

### 1A. Remove all `__internal` export hacks (7 files)

These are test-only export backdoors (`export const __internal = { fn1, fn2 }`). Fix by either making functions proper exports or restructuring tests.

**Files:**
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/controllers/useShotEditorLayoutModel.ts` — 3 functions
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/generateVideoService.ts` — 5 functions
- `supabase/functions/calculate-task-cost/index.ts` — 1 function
- `supabase/functions/huggingface-upload/index.ts` — 4 functions

**Approach per function:**
- Pure/stateless → export directly (drop `__internal` wrapper)
- Tightly coupled → test through public API, delete the extraction
- Delete all `__internal` objects

### 1B. Simplify `buildTravelStructureSource` (148 → ~25 lines)

**File:** `src/shared/lib/tasks/travelContractData.ts`

22 identical `pickFirstDefinedValue` calls. Replace with a field-descriptor loop:

```ts
const STRUCTURE_FIELDS = [
  'structure_guidance', 'structure_videos', 'structure_video_path',
  'structure_video_treatment', 'structure_video_type',
  'structure_video_motion_strength', 'structure_canny_intensity',
  'structure_depth_contrast', 'uni3c_start_percent', 'uni3c_end_percent', 'use_uni3c'
] as const;

function buildTravelStructureSource(snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot): Record<string, unknown> {
  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const field of STRUCTURE_FIELDS) {
    const value = pickFirstDefinedValue(snapshot, field);
    if (value !== undefined) result[field] = value;
  }
  return result;
}
```

### 1C. Inline `useShotEditorLayoutModel` builder functions

**File:** `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/controllers/useShotEditorLayoutModel.ts` (432 lines)

Three single-use builders (`buildShotEditorScreenModel`, `buildShotEditorContextInput`, `buildShotEditorLayoutSections`) exist only for `__internal` testability. Once `__internal` is removed (1A), inline them at their single call sites. Expected: ~221 fewer lines.

### 1D. Restore deleted test coverage

Two test files deleted without replacement in `6fd933f83`:
- `renderModelBuilders.test.tsx` (191 lines)
- `useShotEditorApplySettingsModel.test.tsx` (155 lines)

Write replacement tests covering the same behavior through the new public API.

---

## Part 2: File GitHub Issue on `peteromallet/desloppify`

Create an issue documenting the recurring patterns the execution agent produces that degrade code quality, with evidence from this branch. Title: "Execution agent over-engineering patterns degrading target codebase"

### Issue body structure:

**Problem:** The execution agent (not the review/scoring bot) consistently over-corrects when fixing findings, producing code that is larger and more complex than before.

**Recurring patterns observed (with evidence from `chore/desloppify-queue-2026-03-02`):**

1. **Single-consumer extractions** — Creates new files/functions with exactly 1 caller
   - `toolSettingsAuthCache.ts`, `toolSettingsErrors.ts`, `toolSettingsOperationFailures.ts`, `toolSettingsScopeReader.ts` — all had 0-1 external consumers, added +155 lines of overhead (reverted)
   - `buildShotEditorControllerSlices`, `buildShotEditorSections` + 117 lines of interface defs, each called once (reverted)

2. **`__internal` test export hacks** — 7 files use `export const __internal = {...}` to expose private functions for testing, instead of exporting properly or testing through public API

3. **Naming convention churn** — 8 functions renamed with `OrThrow` suffix; none had a non-throwing variant; pure noise across 18 files (reverted)

4. **Stripping "why" comments** — Navigator.locks rationale, "IMPORTANT: update these transformers" warnings, per-field JSDoc on `HiresFixConfig` — all stripped as collateral during moves (restored)

5. **Behavioral changes hidden in refactors** — `resolvePrimaryStructureVideo` dropped legacy field fallback chain in a "unify contracts" commit; test rewritten to match new behavior rather than preserving old (fixed)

6. **Net line growth while claiming cleanup** — 4 of 5 commits in one batch added lines (+440, +17, +90, +38); only 1 reduced lines (-20)

7. **Repetitive boilerplate** — `buildTravelStructureSource` has 22 identical `pickFirstDefinedValue` calls in 148 lines, could be a 10-line loop

**Suggested constraints for the execution agent:**
- Net lines must decrease or stay flat
- Don't extract unless 2+ consumers exist
- Don't create new files for single-consumer code
- Never use `__internal` exports
- Preserve "why" comments; only strip "what" comments
- Refactors must preserve behavior (no test expectation changes)
- Don't rename for convention alone
- Don't delete tests without replacement

---

## Execution Order

1. **1A + 1C** — Remove `__internal` exports + inline layout model builders
2. **1B** — Simplify `buildTravelStructureSource`
3. **1D** — Restore test coverage
4. **Part 2** — Create GitHub issue on `peteromallet/desloppify`
5. Commit code changes and push

- `npx vitest run` — no new test failures

- Net line count is lower than before
- GitHub issue created and URL returned

   407→  const handleJoinSegmentsClick = useCallback(() => {
   408→    controllers.joinWorkflow.setGenerateMode('join');
   409→    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   410→      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   411→        const target = sections.refs.generateVideosCardRef.current;
   412→        if (!target) {
   413→          return;
   414→        }
   415→

   417→        const scrollTop = window.scrollY + rect.top - 20;
   418→        window.scrollTo({ top: scrollTop, behavior: 'smooth' });
   419→      });
   420→    });
   421→  }, [controllers.joinWorkflow, sections.refs.generateVideosCardRef]);
   422→
   423→  return buildShotEditorLayoutSections({
   424→    core,
   425→    controllers,
   426→    settings,
   427→    sections,
   428→    contextValue,
   429→    handleJoinSegmentsClick,
   430→  });
   431→}
   432→

   127→    const subTaskOrchestratorId = getSubTaskOrchestratorId(typedTask.params, typedTask.id);
   128→
   129→    if (subTaskOrchestratorId) {

   142→    // Check if this is an orchestrator task - calculate cost based on sub-task durations
   143→    let subTasks: CompletedSubTaskRow[] = [];

   146→    } catch (subTaskLookupError) {
   147→      const stage = subTaskLookupError instanceof SubTaskLookupError
   148→        ? subTaskLookupError.stage
   149→        : 'unknown';

   151→        ? 'subtask_query_failed_canonical'

   153→          ? 'subtask_query_failed_legacy'
   154→          : 'subtask_query_failed';
   155→      logger.error("Failed to query sub-task references", {

   158→          : String(subTaskLookupError),
   159→        stage,
   160→      });
   161→      return errorResponse(errorCode, 'Failed to query sub-task references', 500, true);
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    let durationSeconds;
   165→    if (subTasks && subTasks.length > 0) {
   166→      // This is an orchestrator task with sub-tasks - sum their durations
   167→      logger.info("Orchestrator task detected", { sub_task_count: subTasks.length });
   168→
   169→      let totalSubTaskDuration = 0;
   170→      for (const subTask of subTasks) {
   171→        if (subTask.generation_started_at && subTask.generation_processed_at) {
   172→          const subStartTime = new Date(subTask.generation_started_at);
   173→          const subEndTime = new Date(subTask.generation_processed_at);
   174→          const subDuration = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((subEndTime.getTime() - subStartTime.getTime()) / 1000));
   175→          totalSubTaskDuration += subDuration;
   176→        }
   177→      }
   178→
   179→      durationSeconds = totalSubTaskDuration;

   189→    }

   191→    // Idempotency: avoid double-billing if this function is called multiple times for the same task.

   193→    const { data: existingSpendEntries, error: existingSpendError } = await supabaseAdmin

   204→    }

   206→    if (existingSpendEntries && existingSpendEntries.length > 0) {
   207→      const existing = existingSpendEntries[0];
   208→      logger.info("Cost already recorded - skipping", {
   209→        ledger_id: existing.id,
   210→        existing_amount: existing.amount
   211→      });
   212→      return jsonResponse({
   213→        success: true,
   214→        skipped: true,
   215→        reason: 'Cost already recorded for this task',
   216→        task_id: typedTask.id,
   217→        ledger_id: existing.id,
   218→        existing_amount: existing.amount
   219→      });
   220→    }

   223→    const taskType = typedTask.task_type_config;

   227→

   229→      const defaultCostPerSecond = 0.0278;

   231→
   232→      const { error: ledgerError } = await supabaseAdmin.from('credits_ledger').insert({
   233→        user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   234→        task_id: typedTask.id,
   235→        amount: -cost,
   236→        type: 'spend',
   237→        metadata: {
   238→          task_type: typedTask.task_type,
   239→          duration_seconds: durationSeconds,

   241→          billing_type: 'per_second',
   242→          calculated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   243→          cost_fallback_used: true,
   244→          note: 'Default cost used - no task type configuration found'
   245→        }
   246→      });

   250→        return errorResponse('ledger_write_failed', 'Failed to record cost in ledger', 500, true);
   251→      }
   252→
   253→      logger.info("Cost calculated (default rates)", {

   255→        duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   256→        billing_type: 'per_second'

   258→
   259→      return jsonResponse({

   262→        duration_seconds: durationSeconds,

   264→        billing_type: 'per_second',
   265→        note: 'Default cost used - no task type configuration found'
   266→      });
   267→    }
   268→
   269→    // Calculate cost based on task type configuration
   270→    const costResult = calculateTaskCost(

   274→      taskType.unit_cost,

   279→    const cost = costResult.cost;

   281→
   282→    // Validate cost calculation

   284→      logger.error("Invalid cost calculated", {
   285→        cost,

   287→        base_cost_per_second: taskType.base_cost_per_second,
   288→        unit_cost: taskType.unit_cost,

   290→        breakdown: costBreakdown
   291→      });
   292→      return errorResponse('invalid_cost_calculation', 'Invalid cost calculation', 500);
   293→    }
   294→
   295→    // Ensure user exists before inserting credit ledger entry
   296→    const { data: user, error: userError } = await supabaseAdmin

   299→      .eq('id', typedTask.projects.user_id)
   300→      .single();
   301→
   302→    if (userError || !user) {
   303→      logger.error("User not found for credit ledger", { 
   304→        user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id, 
   305→        error: userError?.message 
   306→      });
   307→      return errorResponse('billing_user_not_found', 'User not found for credit calculation', 400);
   308→    }
   309→
   310→    // Insert cost into credit ledger
   311→    const { error: ledgerError } = await supabaseAdmin.from('credits_ledger').insert({

   332→      logger.error("Failed to insert into credit ledger", {

   334→        user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   335→        cost
   336→      });
   337→      return errorResponse('ledger_write_failed', 'Failed to record cost in ledger', 500, true);
   338→    }
   339→
   340→    logger.info("Cost calculated and recorded", {
   341→      cost,
   342→      billing_type: taskType.billing_type,
   343→      duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   344→      user_id: typedTask.projects.user_id,
   345→      ...(costBreakdown ? { breakdown: costBreakdown } : {})
   346→    });
   347→
   348→    return jsonResponse({
   349→      success: true,
   350→      cost: cost,
   351→      billing_type: taskType.billing_type,
   352→      duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
   353→      base_cost_per_second: taskType.base_cost_per_second,
   354→      unit_cost: taskType.unit_cost,
   355→      cost_factors: taskType.cost_factors,
   356→      task_type: typedTask.task_type,
   357→      task_id: typedTask.id,
   358→      // Include breakdown for compound pricing (e.g., video_enhance)
   359→      ...(costBreakdown ? { cost_breakdown: costBreakdown } : {})
   360→    });
   361→
   362→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   363→    const errorMessage = toErrorMessage(error);
   364→    const errorStack = error instanceof Error ? error.stack?.substring(0, 500) : undefined;
   365→    logger.critical("Unexpected error", { error: errorMessage, stack: errorStack });
   366→    return errorResponse('internal_server_error', 'Internal server error', 500, false);
   367→  }});
   368→});

   191→
   192→  const loraDetailsResult = parseJsonField<LoraDetails>(loraDetailsRaw, "loraDetails");
   193→  if (!loraDetailsResult.ok) {
   194→    return loraDetailsResult;
   195→  }
   196→
   197→  const sampleVideosResult = parseSampleVideos(formData.get("sampleVideos") as string | null);
   198→  if (!sampleVideosResult.ok) {
   199→    return sampleVideosResult;
   200→  }
   201→
   202→  return {
   203→    ok: true,
   204→    value: {
   205→      storagePaths,
   206→      loraDetails: loraDetailsResult.value,
   207→      sampleVideos: sampleVideosResult.value,
   208→      repoNameOverride: formData.get("repoName") as string | null,
   209→      isPrivate: formData.get("isPrivate") === "true",
   210→    },
   211→  };
   212→}
   213→
   214→async function normalizeOwnedInputs(
   215→  parsedRequest: ParsedUploadRequest,
   216→  userId: string,
   217→  logger: SystemLogger,
   218→): Promise<ResponseResult<NormalizedUploadRequest>> {
   219→  const normalizedStoragePaths: LoraStoragePaths = {};
   220→  for (const [fileType, path] of Object.entries(parsedRequest.storagePaths)) {
   221→    if (!path) {
   222→      continue;
   223→    }
   224→    const ownedPath = ensureOwnedStoragePath(path, userId, logger);
   225→    if (!ownedPath.ok) {
   226→      await logger.flush();
   227→      return ownedPath;
   228→    }
   229→    normalizedStoragePaths[fileType as keyof LoraStoragePaths] = ownedPath.storagePath;
   230→  }
   231→
   232→  const normalizedSampleVideos: NormalizedSampleVideo[] = [];
   233→  for (const video of parsedRequest.sampleVideos) {
   234→    if (!video || typeof video.originalFileName !== "string") {
   235→      logger.error("Invalid sample video payload", { sampleVideo: video });
   236→      await logger.flush();
   237→      return invalidUploadRequest("Invalid sample video payload");
   238→    }
   239→
   240→    const ownedPath = ensureOwnedStoragePath(video.storagePath, userId, logger);
   241→    if (!ownedPath.ok) {
   242→      await logger.flush();
   243→      return ownedPath;
   244→    }
   245→
   246→    normalizedSampleVideos.push({
   247→      originalFileName: video.originalFileName,
   248→      storagePath: ownedPath.storagePath,
   249→    });
   250→  }
   251→
   252→  return {
   253→    ok: true,
   254→    value: {
   255→      ...parsedRequest,
   256→      storagePaths: normalizedStoragePaths,
   257→      sampleVideos: normalizedSampleVideos,
   258→    },
   259→  };
   260→}
   261→
   262→function logNormalizedUploadRequest(
   263→  logger: SystemLogger,
   264→  request: NormalizedUploadRequest,
   265→): void {
   266→  logger.info("Processing LoRA", {
   267→    name: request.loraDetails.name,
   268→    single: request.storagePaths.single || "none",
   269→    highNoise: request.storagePaths.highNoise || "none",
   270→    lowNoise: request.storagePaths.lowNoise || "none",
   271→    sampleVideos: request.sampleVideos.length,
   272→  });
   273→}
   274→
   275→async function fetchHuggingFaceToken(
   276→  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseClient,
   277→  userId: string,
   278→): Promise<ResponseResult<string>> {
   279→  const { data: apiKeyData, error: apiKeyError } = await supabaseAdmin.rpc(
   280→    "get_external_api_key_decrypted",
   281→    { p_user_id: userId, p_service: "huggingface" },
   282→  );
   283→
   284→  if (apiKeyError || !apiKeyData || apiKeyData.length === 0) {
   285→    return {
   286→      ok: false,
   287→      response: createResponse({
   288→        error: "HuggingFace API key not found. Please set up your HuggingFace token first.",
   289→        code: "HF_TOKEN_NOT_FOUND",
   290→      }, 400),
   291→    };
   292→  }
   293→
   294→  const hfToken = apiKeyData[0]?.key_value;
   295→  if (!hfToken) {
   296→    return {
   297→      ok: false,
   298→      response: createResponse({
   299→        error: "HuggingFace API key is empty. Please re-enter your token.",
   300→        code: "HF_TOKEN_EMPTY",
   301→      }, 400),
   302→    };
   303→  }
   304→
   305→  return { ok: true, value: hfToken };
   306→}
   307→
   308→async function ensureHfRepo(
   309→  huggingFaceHub: HuggingFaceHubClient,
   310→  request: NormalizedUploadRequest,
   311→  hfToken: string,
   312→  logger: SystemLogger,
   313→): Promise<ResponseResult<{ repoId: string }>> {
   314→  const hfUser = await huggingFaceHub.whoAmI({ credentials: { accessToken: hfToken } });
   315→  if (!hfUser.name) {
   316→    return invalidUploadRequest("Could not determine HuggingFace username");
   317→  }
   318→
   319→  const repoName = request.repoNameOverride || sanitizeRepoName(request.loraDetails.name);
   320→  const repoId = `${hfUser.name}/${repoName}`;
   321→
   322→  logger.info("Creating repo", { repoId, isPrivate: request.isPrivate });
   323→  try {
   324→    await huggingFaceHub.createRepo({
   325→      repo: repoId,
   326→      private: request.isPrivate,
   327→      credentials: { accessToken: hfToken },
   328→    });
   329→    logger.info("Repository created", { repoId });
   330→  } catch (repoError: unknown) {
   331→    const repoErrorMessage = toErrorMessage(repoError);
   332→    if (!repoErrorMessage.toLowerCase().includes("already exists")) {
   333→      logger.error("Repo creation error", { error: repoErrorMessage });
   334→      throw repoError;
   335→    }
   336→    logger.info("Repository already exists", { repoId });
   337→  }
   338→
   339→  return { ok: true, value: { repoId } };
   340→}
   341→
   342→/**
   343→ * Sanitize a string to be a valid HuggingFace repository name
   344→ */
   345→function sanitizeRepoName(name: string): string {
   346→  return name
   347→    .toLowerCase()
   348→    .replace(/\s+/g, "-")
   349→    .replace(/[^a-z0-9-._]/g, "")
   350→    .replace(/--+/g, "-")
   351→    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
   352→    .substring(0, 96); // HF has a 96 char limit for repo names
   353→}
   354→
   355→/**
   356→ * Extract original filename from storage path
   357→ * Format: {user_id}/{uuid}-{original_filename}
   358→ */
   359→function extractOriginalFilename(storagePath: string): string {
   360→  const pathParts = storagePath.split("/");
   361→  const fileNameWithUuid = pathParts[pathParts.length - 1];
   362→  return fileNameWithUuid.includes("-")
   363→    ? fileNameWithUuid.substring(fileNameWithUuid.indexOf("-") + 1)
   364→    : fileNameWithUuid;
   365→}
   366→
   367→function buildWorkflowContext(
   368→  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseClient,
   369→  logger: SystemLogger,
   370→  uploadFile: HuggingFaceHubClient["uploadFile"],
   371→  repo: HuggingFaceRepoContext,
   372→): UploadWorkflowContext {
   373→  return {
   374→    supabaseAdmin,
   375→    logger,
   376→    uploadFile,
   377→    repoId: repo.repoId,
   378→    hfToken: repo.hfToken,
   379→    pathsToClean: [],
   380→  };
   381→}
   382→
   383→function buildHuggingFaceAssetUrl(repoId: string, path: string): string {
   384→  return `https://huggingface.co/${repoId}/resolve/main/${encodeURIComponent(path)}`;
   385→}
   386→
   387→async function uploadLoraFile(
   388→  workflow: UploadWorkflowContext,
   389→  storagePath: string,
   390→  fileType: keyof UploadedLoraFiles,
   391→): Promise<string> {
   392→  workflow.logger.info(`Downloading ${fileType} LoRA`, { storagePath });
   393→  const { data: loraBlob, error: loraDownloadError } = await workflow.supabaseAdmin.storage
   394→    .from("temporary")
   395→    .download(storagePath);
   396→
   397→  if (loraDownloadError || !loraBlob) {
   398→    workflow.logger.error(`${fileType} LoRA download error`, { error: loraDownloadError?.message });
   399→    throw new Error(`Failed to download ${fileType} LoRA from temporary storage`);
   400→  }
   401→
   402→  workflow.pathsToClean.push(storagePath);
   403→
   404→  const originalName = extractOriginalFilename(storagePath);
   405→  const loraFile = new File([loraBlob], originalName, { type: "application/octet-stream" });
   406→  workflow.logger.info(`${fileType} LoRA file prepared`, { name: loraFile.name, size: loraFile.size });
   407→
   408→  await workflow.uploadFile({
   409→    repo: workflow.repoId,
   410→    file: loraFile,
   411→    credentials: { [REDACTED] },
   412→  });
   413→  workflow.logger.info(`${fileType} LoRA file uploaded successfully`);
   414→
   415→  return originalName;
   416→}
   417→
   418→async function uploadLoraAssets(
   419→  workflow: UploadWorkflowContext,
   420→  storagePaths: LoraStoragePaths,
   421→): Promise<{ files: UploadedLoraFiles; urls: UploadedLoraUrls }> {
   422→  const files: UploadedLoraFiles = {};
   423→  const urls: UploadedLoraUrls = {};
   424→
   425→  if (storagePaths.single) {
   426→    const filename = await uploadLoraFile(workflow, storagePaths.single, "single");
   427→    files.single = filename;
   428→    urls.loraUrl = buildHuggingFaceAssetUrl(workflow.repoId, filename);
   429→  }
   430→
   431→  if (storagePaths.highNoise) {
   432→    const filename = await uploadLoraFile(workflow, storagePaths.highNoise, "highNoise");
   433→    files.highNoise = filename;
   434→    urls.highNoiseUrl = buildHuggingFaceAssetUrl(workflow.repoId, filename);
   435→  }
   436→
   437→  if (storagePaths.lowNoise) {
   438→    const filename = await uploadLoraFile(workflow, storagePaths.lowNoise, "lowNoise");
   439→    files.lowNoise = filename;
   440→    urls.lowNoiseUrl = buildHuggingFaceAssetUrl(workflow.repoId, filename);
   441→  }
   442→
   443→  return { files, urls };
   444→}
   445→
   446→async function uploadSampleVideos(
   447→  workflow: UploadWorkflowContext,
   448→  sampleVideos: NormalizedSampleVideo[],
   449→): Promise<string[]> {
   450→  const uploadedVideoPaths: string[] = [];
   451→
   452→  for (const video of sampleVideos) {
   453→    try {
   454→      workflow.logger.info("Downloading video", { storagePath: video.storagePath });
   455→      const { data: videoBlob, error: videoError } = await workflow.supabaseAdmin.storage
   456→        .from("temporary")
   457→        .download(video.storagePath);
   458→
   459→      if (videoError || !videoBlob) {
   460→        workflow.logger.error("Video download error", { error: videoError?.message });
   461→        continue;
   462→      }
   463→
   464→      workflow.pathsToClean.push(video.storagePath);
   465→
   466→      const videoFile = new File([videoBlob], video.originalFileName, { type: videoBlob.type });
   467→      const targetPath = `media/${sanitizeRepoName(video.originalFileName)}`;
   468→
   469→      workflow.logger.info("Uploading video", { targetPath });
   470→      await workflow.uploadFile({
   471→        repo: workflow.repoId,
   472→        file: { path: targetPath, content: videoFile },
   473→        credentials: { [REDACTED] },
   474→      });
   475→
   476→      uploadedVideoPaths.push(targetPath);
   477→      workflow.logger.info("Video uploaded", { targetPath });
   478→    } catch (videoUploadError: unknown) {
   479→      workflow.logger.error("Video upload error", { error: toErrorMessage(videoUploadError) });
   480→    }
   481→  }
   482→
   483→  return uploadedVideoPaths;
   484→}
   485→
   486→async function uploadReadme(
   487→  workflow: UploadWorkflowContext,
   488→  loraDetails: LoraDetails,
   489→  loraFiles: UploadedLoraFiles,
   490→  sampleVideoPaths: string[],
   491→): Promise<void> {

   500→  const readmeFile = new File([readmeBlob], "README.md", { type: "text/markdown" });
   501→
   502→  await workflow.uploadFile({
   503→    repo: workflow.repoId,
   504→    file: readmeFile,
   505→    credentials: { [REDACTED] },
   506→  });
   507→  workflow.logger.info("README uploaded");
   508→}
   509→
   510→async function cleanupTemporaryAssets(workflow: UploadWorkflowContext): Promise<void> {
   511→  workflow.logger.info("Cleaning up temporary files", { count: workflow.pathsToClean.length });
   512→  if (workflow.pathsToClean.length === 0) {
   513→    return;
   514→  }
   515→
   516→  const { error: cleanupError } = await workflow.supabaseAdmin.storage
   517→    .from("temporary")
   518→    .remove(workflow.pathsToClean);
   519→
   520→  if (cleanupError) {
   521→    workflow.logger.error("Cleanup error", { error: cleanupError.message });
   522→    return;
   523→  }
   524→
   525→  workflow.logger.info("Cleanup successful");
   526→}
   527→
   528→function buildUploadResponse(
   529→  repoId: string,
   530→  uploadedLoraUrls: UploadedLoraUrls,
   531→  uploadedVideoPaths: string[],
   532→) {
   533→  return {
   534→    success: true,
   535→    repoId,
   536→    repoUrl: `https://huggingface.co/${repoId}`,
   537→    ...uploadedLoraUrls,
   538→    videoUrls: uploadedVideoPaths.map((path) => buildHuggingFaceAssetUrl(repoId, path)),
   539→  };

   541→
   542→/**
   543→ * Generate README.md content for the HuggingFace model card
   544→ * Supports both single-stage and multi-stage LoRAs
   545→ */
   546→function generateReadmeContent(
   547→  loraDetails: {
   548→    name: string;
   549→    description?: string;
   550→    baseModel: string;
   551→    triggerWord?: string;
   552→    creatorName?: string;
   553→  },
   554→  loraFiles: {
   555→    single?: string;      // filename for single-stage
   556→    highNoise?: string;   // filename for multi-stage high noise
   557→    lowNoise?: string;    // filename for multi-stage low noise
   558→  },
   559→  repoId: string,
   560→  sampleVideoPaths: string[]
   561→): string {
   562→  let readme = "";
   563→
   564→  // YAML frontmatter for HuggingFace model card
   565→  readme += "---\n";
   566→  readme += "tags:\n";
   567→  readme += "- lora\n";
   568→  readme += "- video-generation\n";
   569→
   570→  // Add base model tag
   571→  const baseModelLower = loraDetails.baseModel.toLowerCase();
   572→  if (baseModelLower.includes("wan")) {
   573→    readme += "- wan\n";
   574→    if (baseModelLower.includes("i2v")) readme += "- image-to-video\n";
   575→    if (baseModelLower.includes("t2v")) readme += "- text-to-video\n";
   576→    if (baseModelLower.includes("2.2")) readme += "- multi-stage\n";
   577→  } else if (baseModelLower.includes("qwen")) {
   578→    readme += "- qwen\n";
   579→    readme += "- image-generation\n";
   580→  }
   581→
   582→  // Widget for video previews
   583→  if (sampleVideoPaths.length > 0) {
   584→    readme += "widget:\n";
   585→    sampleVideoPaths.forEach((videoPath) => {
   586→      readme += `- output:\n`;
   587→      readme += `    url: ${videoPath}\n`;
   588→    });
   589→  }
   590→  readme += "---\n\n";
   591→
   592→  // Main content
   593→  readme += `# ${loraDetails.name}\n\n`;
   594→  readme += `This LoRA was uploaded via [Reigh](https://reigh.ai/).\n\n`;
   595→
   596→  readme += `## Model Details\n\n`;
   597→
   598→  // Show files based on single vs multi-stage
   599→  if (loraFiles.highNoise || loraFiles.lowNoise) {
   600→    // Multi-stage LoRA
   601→    if (loraFiles.highNoise) {
   602→      readme += `**High Noise File:** \`${loraFiles.highNoise}\` ([Download](https://huggingface.co/${repoId}/resolve/main/${encodeURIComponent(loraFiles.highNoise)}))\n\n`;
   603→    }
   604→    if (loraFiles.lowNoise) {
   605→      readme += `**Low Noise File:** \`${loraFiles.lowNoise}\` ([Download](https://huggingface.co/${repoId}/resolve/main/${encodeURIComponent(loraFiles.lowNoise)}))\n\n`;
   606→    }
   607→  } else if (loraFiles.single) {
   608→    // Single-stage LoRA
   609→    readme += `**File:** \`${loraFiles.single}\` ([Download](https://huggingface.co/${repoId}/resolve/main/${encodeURIComponent(loraFiles.single)}))\n\n`;
   610→  }
   611→
   612→  readme += `**Base Model:** ${loraDetails.baseModel}\n\n`;
   613→
   614→  if (loraDetails.triggerWord) {
   615→    readme += `**Trigger Word:** \`${loraDetails.triggerWord}\`\n\n`;
   616→  }
   617→
   618→  if (loraDetails.description) {
   619→    readme += `## Description\n\n`;
   620→    readme += `${loraDetails.description}\n\n`;
   621→  }
   622→
   623→  if (loraDetails.creatorName) {
   624→    readme += `## Creator\n\n`;
   625→    readme += `Created by: **${loraDetails.creatorName}**\n\n`;
   626→  }
   627→
   628→  if (sampleVideoPaths.length > 0) {
   629→    readme += `## Examples\n\n`;
   630→    sampleVideoPaths.forEach((videoPath, index) => {
   631→      const fileName = videoPath.split("/").pop() || `Example ${index + 1}`;
   632→      readme += `- [${fileName}](./${videoPath})\n`;
   633→    });
   634→    readme += "\n";
   635→  }
   636→
   637→  return readme;
   638→}
   639→
   640→/**
   641→ * Edge function: huggingface-upload
   642→ *
   643→ * Uploads LoRA files and sample videos to HuggingFace on behalf of the user.
   644→ * Supports both single-stage (one file) and multi-stage (high_noise + low_noise) LoRAs.
   645→ *
   646→ * Flow:
   647→ * 1. Authenticate user via Supabase JWT
   648→ * 2. Retrieve user's HuggingFace token from external_api_keys table
   649→ * 3. Download files from temporary storage bucket
   650→ * 4. Create HF repo and upload files
   651→ * 5. Generate README.md
   652→ * 6. Clean up temporary files
   653→ * 7. Return HuggingFace URLs
   654→ */
   655→export const __internal = {
   656→  buildUploadResponse,
   657→  normalizeOwnedInputs,
   658→  parseUploadRequest,
   659→  sanitizeRepoName,
   660→};
   661→
   662→async function handleHuggingFaceUpload(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
   663→  // Handle CORS preflight
   664→  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
   665→    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
   666→  }
   667→  if (req.method !== "POST") {
   668→    return createResponse({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);
   669→  }
   670→
   671→  const bootstrap = await bootstrapEdgeHandler(req, {
   672→    functionName: "huggingface-upload",
   673→    logPrefix: "[HF-UPLOAD]",
   674→    method: "POST",
   675→    parseBody: "none",
   676→    corsPreflight: false,
   677→    auth: {
   678→      required: true,
   679→      options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true },
   680→    },
   681→    ...NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS,
   682→  });
   683→  if (!bootstrap.ok) {
   684→    return bootstrap.response;
   685→  }
   686→
   687→  const { supabaseAdmin, logger, auth } = bootstrap.value;
   688→
   689→  try {
   690→    const huggingFaceHub = await loadHuggingFaceHubClient();
   691→
   692→    // 1. Authenticate user
   693→    const authResult = ensureUserAuth(auth, logger);
   694→    if (!authResult.ok) {
   695→      return createResponse({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);
   696→    }
   697→
   698→    const userId = authResult.userId;
   699→    logger.info("Authenticated request");
   700→
   701→    const parsedRequest = await parseUploadRequest(req);
   702→    if (!parsedRequest.ok) {
   703→      return parsedRequest.response;
   704→    }
   705→
   706→    const normalizedRequest = await normalizeOwnedInputs(parsedRequest.value, userId, logger);
   707→    if (!normalizedRequest.ok) {
   708→      return normalizedRequest.response;
   709→    }
   710→
   711→    logNormalizedUploadRequest(logger, normalizedRequest.value);
   712→
   713→    const tokenResult = await fetchHuggingFaceToken(supabaseAdmin, userId);
   714→    if (!tokenResult.ok) {
   715→      return tokenResult.response;
   716→    }
   717→
   718→    const repoResult = await ensureHfRepo(
   719→      huggingFaceHub,
   720→      normalizedRequest.value,
   721→      tokenResult.value,
   722→      logger,
   723→    );
   724→    if (!repoResult.ok) {
   725→      return repoResult.response;
   726→    }
   727→
   728→    const workflow = buildWorkflowContext(
   729→      supabaseAdmin,
   730→      logger,
   731→      huggingFaceHub.uploadFile,
   732→      { [REDACTED], repoId: repoResult.value.repoId },
   733→    );
   734→
   735→    const uploadedLoraAssets = await uploadLoraAssets(workflow, normalizedRequest.value.storagePaths);
   736→    const uploadedVideoPaths = await uploadSampleVideos(workflow, normalizedRequest.value.sampleVideos);
   737→    await uploadReadme(
   738→      workflow,
   739→      normalizedRequest.value.loraDetails,
   740→      uploadedLoraAssets.files,
   741→      uploadedVideoPaths,
   742→    );
   743→    await cleanupTemporaryAssets(workflow);
   744→
   745→    logger.info("Upload complete", { repoId: workflow.repoId });
   746→    await logger.flush();
   747→
   748→    return createResponse(
   749→      buildUploadResponse(workflow.repoId, uploadedLoraAssets.urls, uploadedVideoPaths),
   750→    );
   751→
   752→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   753→    const message = toErrorMessage(error);
   754→    logger.error("Unexpected error", { error: message });
   755→    await logger.flush();
   756→    return createResponse({
   757→      error: message || "An unexpected error occurred",
   758→    }, 500);
   759→  }
   760→}
   761→
   762→serve(handleHuggingFaceUpload);
   763→
```

    23→const CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY: TravelPayloadSource[] = [
    24→  ...CONTRACT_SOURCES,
    25→  ...LEGACY_SOURCES,
    26→];
    27→
    28→interface TravelContractData {
    29→  inputImages?: string[];
    30→  prompt?: string;
    31→  enhancedPrompt?: string;
    32→  negativePrompt?: string;
    33→  modelName?: string;
    34→  resolution?: string;
    35→  motionMode?: string;
    36→  selectedPhasePresetId?: string;
    37→  advancedMode?: boolean;
    38→  amountOfMotion?: number;

    41→  structureGuidance?: UnknownRecord;
    42→  segmentFramesExpanded?: number[];
    43→  frameOverlapExpanded?: number[];
    44→}
    45→
    46→export interface ResolvedTravelStructureContractData {
    47→  present: boolean;
    48→  structureGuidance?: UnknownRecord;
    49→  structureVideos: ReturnType<typeof resolveTravelStructureState>['structureVideos'];
    50→  primaryStructureVideo: ReturnType<typeof resolvePrimaryStructureVideo>;
    51→}
    52→
    53→function pickFirstDefinedValue(
    54→  reader: ReturnType<typeof createTravelPayloadReader>,
    55→  keys: string[],
    56→  sources: TravelPayloadSource[],
    57→): unknown {
    58→  for (const key of keys) {
    59→    const value = reader.read(key, sources);
    60→    if (value !== undefined) {
    61→      return value;
    62→    }
    63→  }
    64→  return undefined;
    65→}
    66→
    67→function pickRecord(reader: ReturnType<typeof createTravelPayloadReader>, key: string): UnknownRecord | undefined {
    68→  const contractRecord = reader.pickRecord(key, CONTRACT_SOURCES);
    69→  if (contractRecord) {
    70→    return contractRecord;
    71→  }
    72→  const legacyRecord = reader.pickRecord(key, LEGACY_SOURCES);
    73→  return legacyRecord;
    74→}
    75→
    76→export function buildTravelStructureSource(
    77→  snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot,
    78→): Record<string, unknown> {
    79→  const reader = createTravelPayloadReader(snapshot);
    80→  const structureGuidance = pickFirstDefinedValue(
    81→    reader,
    82→    ['structure_guidance', 'structureGuidance'],
    83→    CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY,
    84→  );
    85→  const guidanceVideos = asRecord(structureGuidance)?.videos;
    86→
    87→  return {
    88→    ...(structureGuidance !== undefined
    89→      ? { structure_guidance: structureGuidance }
    90→      : {}),
    91→    ...(pickFirstDefinedValue(
    92→      reader,
    93→      ['structure_videos', 'structureVideos'],
    94→      CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY,
    95→    ) !== undefined
    96→      ? {
    97→          structure_videos: pickFirstDefinedValue(
    98→            reader,
    99→            ['structure_videos', 'structureVideos'],
   100→            CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY,
   101→          ),
   102→        }
   103→      : Array.isArray(guidanceVideos)
   104→        ? { structure_videos: guidanceVideos }
   105→        : {}),
   106→    ...(pickFirstDefinedValue(
   107→      reader,
   108→      ['structure_video_path', 'structureVideoPath'],
   109→      LEGACY_SOURCES,
   110→    ) !== undefined
   111→      ? {
   112→          structure_video_path: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   113→            reader,
   114→            ['structure_video_path', 'structureVideoPath'],
   115→            LEGACY_SOURCES,
   116→          ),
   117→        }
   118→      : {}),
   119→    ...(pickFirstDefinedValue(
   120→      reader,
   121→      ['structure_video_treatment', 'structureVideoTreatment'],
   122→      LEGACY_SOURCES,
   123→    ) !== undefined
   124→      ? {
   125→          structure_video_treatment: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   126→            reader,
   127→            ['structure_video_treatment', 'structureVideoTreatment'],
   128→            LEGACY_SOURCES,
   129→          ),
   130→        }
   131→      : {}),
   132→    ...(pickFirstDefinedValue(
   133→      reader,
   134→      ['structure_video_type', 'structure_type', 'structureVideoType', 'structureType'],
   135→      LEGACY_SOURCES,
   136→    ) !== undefined
   137→      ? {
   138→          structure_video_type: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   139→            reader,
   140→            ['structure_video_type', 'structure_type', 'structureVideoType', 'structureType'],
   141→            LEGACY_SOURCES,
   142→          ),
   143→        }
   144→      : {}),
   145→    ...(pickFirstDefinedValue(
   146→      reader,
   147→      ['structure_video_motion_strength', 'structureVideoMotionStrength'],
   148→      LEGACY_SOURCES,
   149→    ) !== undefined
   150→      ? {
   151→          structure_video_motion_strength: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   152→            reader,
   153→            ['structure_video_motion_strength', 'structureVideoMotionStrength'],
   154→            LEGACY_SOURCES,
   155→          ),
   156→        }
   157→      : {}),
   158→    ...(pickFirstDefinedValue(
   159→      reader,
   160→      ['structure_canny_intensity', 'structureCannyIntensity'],

   162→    ) !== undefined
   163→      ? {
   164→          structure_canny_intensity: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   165→            reader,
   166→            ['structure_canny_intensity', 'structureCannyIntensity'],
   167→            LEGACY_SOURCES,
   168→          ),
   169→        }
   170→      : {}),

   173→      ['structure_depth_contrast', 'structureDepthContrast'],

   176→      ? {

   183→      : {}),

   189→      ? {
   190→          uni3c_start_percent: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   191→            reader,

   196→      : {}),

   202→      ? {

   209→      : {}),

   215→      ? {
   216→          use_uni3c: pickFirstDefinedValue(
   217→            reader,
   218→            ['use_uni3c', 'useUni3c'],
   219→            LEGACY_SOURCES,

   222→      : {}),
   223→  };
   224→}
   225→
   226→export function readResolvedTravelStructure(
   227→  snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot,
   228→): ResolvedTravelStructureContractData {
   229→  const source = buildTravelStructureSource(snapshot);
   230→  const resolved = resolveTravelStructureState(source, {
   231→    defaultEndFrame: 0,
   232→    defaultVideoTreatment: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_VIDEO.treatment,
   233→    defaultMotionStrength: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.motionStrength,
   234→    defaultStructureType: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.structureType,
   235→    defaultUni3cEndPercent: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.uni3cEndPercent,
   236→  });
   237→
   238→  return {
   239→    present: Object.keys(source).length > 0,
   240→    structureGuidance: resolved.structureGuidance,
   241→    structureVideos: resolved.structureVideos,
   242→    primaryStructureVideo: resolvePrimaryStructureVideo(
   243→      resolved.structureVideos,
   244→      resolved.structureGuidance,
   245→    ),
   246→  };
   247→}
   248→
   249→export function readTravelContractData(snapshot: TaskPayloadSnapshot): TravelContractData {
   250→  const reader = createTravelPayloadReader(snapshot);
   251→  const structureGuidance = pickRecord(reader, 'structure_guidance');
   252→  const phaseConfig = pickRecord(reader, 'phase_config');
   253→
   254→  return {
   255→    inputImages: reader.pickStringArray('input_images', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY)
   256→      ?? reader.pickStringArray('input_image_paths_resolved', LEGACY_SOURCES),
   257→    prompt: reader.pickString('prompt', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY)
   258→      ?? reader.pickString('base_prompt', LEGACY_SOURCES),
   259→    enhancedPrompt: reader.pickString('enhanced_prompt', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   260→    negativePrompt: reader.pickString('negative_prompt', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   261→    modelName: reader.pickString('model_name', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   262→    resolution: reader.pickString('resolution', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY)
   263→      ?? reader.pickString('parsed_resolution_wh', LEGACY_SOURCES),
   264→    motionMode: reader.pickString('motion_mode', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   265→    selectedPhasePresetId: reader.pickString('selected_phase_preset_id', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   266→    advancedMode: reader.pickBoolean('advanced_mode', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   267→    amountOfMotion: reader.pickNumber('amount_of_motion', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   268→    phaseConfig,
   269→    additionalLoras: pickRecord(reader, 'additional_loras'),
   270→    structureGuidance,
   271→    segmentFramesExpanded: reader.pickNumberArray('segment_frames_expanded', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   272→    frameOverlapExpanded: reader.pickNumberArray('frame_overlap_expanded', CONTRACT_THEN_LEGACY),
   273→  };
   274→}

    50→ * Build unified structure_guidance object for the API request.

    52→ */
    53→function buildStructureGuidance(
    54→  structureGuidanceOrVideos: Record<string, unknown> | StructureVideoConfigWithMetadata[] | undefined,
    55→  structureVideosArg?: StructureVideoConfigWithMetadata[] | undefined,
    56→): Record<string, unknown> | null {

    58→    ? undefined
    59→    : structureGuidanceOrVideos;
    60→  const structureVideos = Array.isArray(structureGuidanceOrVideos)
    61→    ? structureGuidanceOrVideos

    70→    defaultUni3cEndPercent: DEFAULT_STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE_CONTROLS.uni3cEndPercent,
    71→  });
    72→  return normalized ?? null;

    76→const SHOT_GEN_QUERY = `

    89→function failureResult(error: unknown, options: RuntimeErrorOptions): GenerateVideoResult {
    90→  const appError = normalizeAndPresentError(error, options);
    91→  return operationFailure(appError, {
    92→    errorCode: 'generate_video_failed',

    94→    recoverable: true,

   103→
   104→function resolveGenerationResolution(selectedShot: Shot, effectiveAspectRatio: string | null): string {
   105→  if (selectedShot?.aspect_ratio) {
   106→    const shotResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[selectedShot.aspect_ratio];
   107→    if (shotResolution) return shotResolution;
   108→  }
   109→  if (effectiveAspectRatio) {
   110→    const projectResolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[effectiveAspectRatio];
   111→    if (projectResolution) return projectResolution;
   112→  }
   113→  return DEFAULT_RESOLUTION;
   114→}
   115→
   116→async function waitForPendingMutations(queryClient: QueryClient, timeoutMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
   117→  if (queryClient.isMutating() === 0) return;
   118→
   119→  const start = Date.now();
   120→  while (queryClient.isMutating() > 0) {
   121→    if (Date.now() - start > timeoutMs) break;
   122→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
   123→  }
   124→}
   125→
   126→async function fetchFreshShotGenerations(selectedShotId: string): Promise<ShotGenRow[]> {
   127→  const { data, error } = await supabase().from('shot_generations')
   128→    .select(SHOT_GEN_QUERY)
   129→    .eq('shot_id', selectedShotId)
   130→    .order('timeline_frame', { ascending: true });
   131→
   132→  if (error) throw error;
   133→  return (data || []) as ShotGenRow[];
   134→}

   157→
   158→/**
   159→ * Prepare and submit a video generation task.
   160→ * Handles resolution, fresh DB queries, per-pair overrides, model selection,
   161→ * structure guidance, and task creation.
   162→ */
   163→export async function generateVideo(params: GenerateVideoParams): Promise<GenerateVideoResult> {
   164→  const {
   165→    projectId,
   166→    selectedShotId,
   167→    selectedShot,
   168→    queryClient,
   169→    effectiveAspectRatio,
   170→    generationMode,
   171→    promptConfig,
   172→    motionConfig,
   173→    modelConfig,
   174→    batchVideoFrames,
   175→    selectedLoras,
   176→    variantNameParam,
   177→    clearAllEnhancedPrompts,
   178→    parentGenerationId,
   179→    stitchConfig,
   180→    structureGuidance,
   181→    structureVideos,
   182→  } = params;
   183→
   184→  if (!projectId) {
   185→    return failureResult(
   186→      new ValidationError('No project selected. Please select a project first.', { field: 'projectId' }),
   187→      { context: 'generateVideoService', toastTitle: 'No project selected' },
   188→    );
   189→  }
   190→
   191→  await waitForPendingMutations(queryClient);
   192→  const resolution = resolveGenerationResolution(selectedShot, effectiveAspectRatio);
   193→  const amountOfMotion = motionConfig.amount_of_motion ?? 50;
   194→
   195→  let allShotGenerations: ShotGenRow[];
   196→  try {
   197→    allShotGenerations = await fetchFreshShotGenerations(selectedShotId);
   198→  } catch (error) {
   199→    return failureResult(error, {
   200→      context: 'TaskSubmission',
   201→      toastTitle: 'Failed to fetch current images',
   202→    });
   203→  }
   204→
   205→  const imagePayload = buildImagePayload(allShotGenerations);
   206→  const pairConfig = (() => {
   207→    switch (generationMode) {
   208→      case 'timeline':
   209→        return buildTimelinePairConfig(allShotGenerations, batchVideoFrames, promptConfig.default_negative_prompt);
   210→      case 'by-pair':
   211→        return buildByPairConfig(allShotGenerations, batchVideoFrames, promptConfig.default_negative_prompt);
   212→      case 'batch':
   213→      default:
   214→        return buildBatchPairConfig(imagePayload.absoluteImageUrls, batchVideoFrames, promptConfig.default_negative_prompt);
   215→    }
   216→  })();
   217→
   218→  const modelPhaseSelection = resolveModelPhaseSelection(
   219→    amountOfMotion,
   220→    motionConfig.advanced_mode,
   221→    motionConfig.phase_config,
   222→    motionConfig.motion_mode,
   223→    selectedLoras,
   224→  );
   225→
   226→  try {
   227→    validatePhaseConfigConsistency(modelPhaseSelection.effectivePhaseConfig);
   228→  } catch (error) {
   229→    return failureResult(error, {
   230→      context: 'generateVideoService',
   231→      toastTitle: 'Invalid phase configuration',
   232→    });
   233→  }
   234→
   235→  const requestBody = buildTravelRequestBodyV2({
   236→    projectId,
   237→    selectedShot,
   238→    imagePayload,
   239→    pairConfig,
   240→    parentGenerationId,
   241→    actualModelName: modelPhaseSelection.actualModelName,
   242→    generationTypeMode: modelConfig.generation_type_mode,
   243→    motionParams: {
   244→      amountOfMotion,
   245→      motionMode: motionConfig.motion_mode,
   246→      useAdvancedMode: modelPhaseSelection.useAdvancedMode,
   247→      effectivePhaseConfig: modelPhaseSelection.effectivePhaseConfig,
   248→      selectedPhasePresetId: motionConfig.selected_phase_preset_id,
   249→    },
   250→    generationParams: {
   251→      generationMode,
   252→      batchVideoPrompt: promptConfig.base_prompt,
   253→      enhancePrompt: promptConfig.enhance_prompt,
   254→      variantNameParam,
   255→      textBeforePrompts: promptConfig.text_before_prompts,
   256→      textAfterPrompts: promptConfig.text_after_prompts,
   257→    },
   258→    seedParams: {
   259→      seed: modelConfig.seed,
   260→      randomSeed: modelConfig.random_seed,
   261→      turboMode: modelConfig.turbo_mode,
   262→      debug: modelConfig.debug,
   263→    },
   264→  });
   265→
   266→  if (selectedLoras && selectedLoras.length > 0) {
   267→    requestBody.loras = selectedLoras.map(lora => ({
   268→      path: lora.path,
   269→      strength: parseFloat(lora.strength?.toString() ?? '1') || 1.0,
   270→    }));
   271→  }
   272→
   273→  requestBody.resolution = resolution;
   274→  if (stitchConfig) requestBody.stitch_config = stitchConfig;
   275→
   276→  const normalizedStructureGuidance = buildStructureGuidance(
   277→    structureGuidance as Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
   278→    structureVideos,
   279→  );
   280→  if (normalizedStructureGuidance) requestBody.structure_guidance = normalizedStructureGuidance;
   281→
   282→  try {
   283→    validateTravelBetweenImagesParams(requestBody);
   284→
   285→    if (!promptConfig.enhance_prompt) {
   286→      try {
   287→        await clearAllEnhancedPrompts();
   288→      } catch (clearError) {
   289→        normalizeAndPresentError(clearError, { context: 'generateVideoService', showToast: false });
   290→      }
   291→    }
   292→
   293→    const result = await createTravelBetweenImagesTaskWithParentGeneration(requestBody);
   294→    return operationSuccess(
   295→      { parentGenerationId: result.parentGenerationId },
   296→      { policy: 'best_effort' },
   297→    );
   298→  } catch (error) {
   299→    return failureResult(error, {
   300→      context: 'generateVideoService',
   301→      toastTitle: 'Failed to create video generation task',
   302→    });
   303→  }
   304→}
   305→

   209→export function useTimelineOrchestrator({

   321→
   322→  const {

sources] of STRUCTURE_FIELDS) {\n    const value = pickFirstDefinedValue(reader, lookupKeys, sources);\n    if (value !== undefined) {\n      result[outputKey] = value;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Fallback: The review bot finds real issues (dead exports, missing tests, duplication), but the execution agent's fixes violate the target codebase's core principle: \"every change should make the codebase smaller or more explicit.\"\n\n## Recurring patterns observed\n\nEvidence from `chore/desloppify-queue-2026-03-02` on `peteromallet/reigh`:\n\n### 1. Single-consumer extractions\nCreates new files/functions with exactly 1 caller:\n- `toolSettingsAuthCache.ts`, `toolSettingsErrors.ts`, `toolSettingsOperationFailures.ts`, `toolSettingsScopeReader.ts` — all had 0-1 external consumers, added +155 lines of overhead (reverted in `bc01baa7f`)\n- `buildShotEditorControllerSlices`, `buildShotEditorSections` + 117 lines of interface defs, each called once (reverted)\n\n### 2. `__internal` test export hacks\n7 files used `export const __internal = {...}` to expose private functions for testing, instead of exporting them properly or testing through the public API. Files affected:\n- `useShotEditorLayoutModel.ts`, `generateVideoService.ts`, `useTimelineOrchestrator.ts`, `calculate-task-cost/index.ts`, `huggingface-upload/index.ts`\n\n### 3. Naming convention churn\n8 functions renamed with `OrThrow` suffix; none had a non-throwing variant; pure noise across 18 files (reverted in `bc01baa7f`)\n\n### 4. Stripping \"why\" comments\nNavigator.locks rationale, \"IMPORTANT: update these transformers\" warnings, per-field JSDoc on `HiresFixConfig` — all stripped as collateral during moves (restored in `7561f7b9e`)\n\n### 5. Behavioral changes hidden in refactors\n`resolvePrimaryStructureVideo` dropped a legacy field fallback chain in a \"unify contracts\" commit; the test was rewritten to match the new behavior rather than preserving the old (fixed in `7561f7b9e`)\n\n### 6. Net line growth while claiming cleanup\n4 of 5 commits in one batch added lines (+440, +17, +90, +38); only 1 reduced lines (-20)\n\n### 7. Repetitive boilerplate\n`buildTravelStructureSource` had 22 identical `pickFirstDefinedValue` calls in 148 lines. A 25-line field-descriptor loop does the same thing.\n\n## Suggested constraints for the execution agent\n\n- **Net lines must decrease or stay flat** — if a cleanup commit adds lines, something is wrong\n- **Don't extract unless 2+ consumers exist** — single-consumer extractions add indirection without value\n- **Don't create new files for single-consumer code** — keep things colocated\n- **Never use `__internal` exports** — either export the function properly or test through the public API\n- **Preserve \"why\" comments; only strip \"what\" comments** — rationale comments are load-bearing\n- **Refactors must preserve behavior** — no test expectation changes in refactor commits\n- **Don't rename for convention alone** — `OrThrow` suffixes, etc. are noise if there's no non-throwing variant\n- **Don't delete tests without replacement** — two test files (191 + 155 lines) were deleted in `6fd933f83` without equivalent coverage\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1",

+export interface PhaseLoraStrength {
+  loraId: string;
+  loraPath: string;
+  loraName: string;
+  pass1Strength: number;
+  pass2Strength: number;
+}
+
+export type ResolutionMode = 'project' | 'custom';
+
+export interface HiresFixConfig {
+  enabled: boolean;
+  resolution_mode: ResolutionMode;
+  custom_aspect_ratio?: string;
+  resolution_scale: number;
+  base_steps: number;
+  hires_scale: number;
+  hires_steps: number;
+  hires_denoise: number;
+  lightning_lora_strength_phase_1: number;
+  lightning_lora_strength_phase_2: number;
+  phaseLoraStrengths: PhaseLoraStrength[];
+}
+
+export const DEFAULT_HIRES_FIX_CONFIG: HiresFixConfig = {
+  enabled: true,
+  resolution_mode: 'project',
+  resolution_scale: 1.5,
+  base_steps: 8,
+  hires_scale: 1.1,
+  hires_steps: 8,
+  hires_denoise: 0.55,
+  lightning_lora_strength_phase_1: 0.9,
+  lightning_lora_strength_phase_2: 0.5,
+  phaseLoraStrengths: [],
+};
+
+const MODEL_HIRES_FIX_DEFAULTS: Record<string, HiresFixConfig> = {
+  'qwen-image': {
+    enabled: true,
+    resolution_mode: 'project',
+    resolution_scale: 1.5,
+    base_steps: 8,
+    hires_scale: 1.1,
+    hires_steps: 8,
+    hires_denoise: 0.55,
+    lightning_lora_strength_phase_1: 0.9,
+    lightning_lora_strength_phase_2: 0.5,
+    phaseLoraStrengths: [],
+  },
+  'qwen-image-2512': {
+    enabled: true,
+    resolution_mode: 'project',
+    resolution_scale: 1.5,
+    base_steps: 10,
+    hires_scale: 1.0,
+    hires_steps: 8,
+    hires_denoise: 0.5,
+    lightning_lora_strength_phase_1: 0.85,
+    lightning_lora_strength_phase_2: 0.4,
+    phaseLoraStrengths: [],
+  },
+  'z-image': {
+    enabled: true,
+    resolution_mode: 'project',
+    resolution_scale: 1.5,
+    base_steps: 12,
+    hires_scale: 1.2,
+    hires_steps: 10,
+    hires_denoise: 0.6,
+    lightning_lora_strength_phase_1: 0.8,
+    lightning_lora_strength_phase_2: 0.3,
+    phaseLoraStrengths: [],
+  },
+};
+
+export function getHiresFixDefaultsForModel(modelName: string): HiresFixConfig {
+  return MODEL_HIRES_FIX_DEFAULTS[modelName] ?? DEFAULT_HIRES_FIX_CONFIG;
+}

diff --git a/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsErrors.ts b/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsErrors.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..9489a91ce
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsErrors.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+import { isCancellationError, getErrorMessage } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
+
+type ToolSettingsErrorCode =
+  | 'auth_required'
+  | 'cancelled'
+  | 'network'
+  | 'scope_fetch_failed'
+  | 'invalid_scope_identifier'
+  | 'unknown';
+
+interface ToolSettingsErrorOptions {
+  recoverable?: boolean;
+  cause?: unknown;
+  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
+}
+
+export class ToolSettingsError extends Error {
+  readonly code: ToolSettingsErrorCode;
+  readonly recoverable: boolean;
+  readonly metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
+  readonly cause?: unknown;
+
+  constructor(
+    code: ToolSettingsErrorCode,
+    message: string,
+    options: ToolSettingsErrorOptions = {},
+  ) {
+    super(message);
+    this.name = 'ToolSettingsError';
+    this.code = code;
+    this.recoverable = options.recoverable ?? false;
+    this.metadata = options.metadata;
+    this.cause = options.cause;
+  }
+}
+
+export function isToolSettingsError(error: unknown): error is ToolSettingsError {
+  return error instanceof ToolSettingsError;
+}
+
+export function classifyToolSettingsError(error: unknown): ToolSettingsError {
+  if (isToolSettingsError(error)) {
+    return error;
+  }
+  if (isCancellationError(error)) {
+    return new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
+      recoverable: true,
+      cause: error,
+    });
+  }
+  const message = getErrorMessage(error);
+  if (message.includes('Authentication required')) {
+    return new ToolSettingsError('auth_required', message, {
+      recoverable: false,
+      cause: error,
+    });
+  }
+  if (
+    message.includes('Failed to fetch')
+    || message.includes('ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES')
+    || message.includes('Network connection issue')
+    || message.includes('Network exhaustion')
+  ) {
+    return new ToolSettingsError('network', message, {
+      recoverable: true,
+      cause: error,
+    });
+  }
+  return new ToolSettingsError('unknown', message, {
+    recoverable: false,
+    cause: error,
+  });
+}
diff --git a/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsOperationFailures.ts b/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsOperationFailures.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..f860cebdf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsOperationFailures.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,98 @@
+import { isCancellationError, getErrorMessage } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
+import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
+import {
+  operationFailure,
+  type OperationFailure,
+} from '@/shared/lib/operationResult';
+import {
+  classifyToolSettingsError,
+  isToolSettingsError,
+  ToolSettingsError,
+} from './toolSettingsErrors';
+
+export function toToolSettingsOperationFailure(error: ToolSettingsError): OperationFailure {
+  return operationFailure(error, {
+    policy: error.recoverable ? 'best_effort' : 'fail_closed',
+    recoverable: error.recoverable,
+    errorCode: error.code,
+    message: error.message,
+    cause: error.cause,
+  });
+}
+
+export function toToolSettingsErrorFromOperationFailure(failure: OperationFailure): ToolSettingsError {
+  const code = failure.errorCode;
+  const normalizedCode = (
+    code === 'auth_required'
+    || code === 'cancelled'
+    || code === 'network'
+    || code === 'scope_fetch_failed'
+    || code === 'invalid_scope_identifier'
+    || code === 'unknown'
+  ) ? code : 'unknown';
+
+  return new ToolSettingsError(normalizedCode, failure.message, {
+    recoverable: failure.recoverable,
+    cause: failure.cause ?? failure.error,
+  });
+}
+
+export function normalizeToolSettingsOperationFailure(error: unknown): OperationFailure {
+  if (isToolSettingsError(error) && error.code === 'cancelled') {
+    return toToolSettingsOperationFailure(error);
+  }
+  if (isCancellationError(error)) {
+    return toToolSettingsOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
+      recoverable: true,
+      cause: error,
+    }));
+  }
+
+  const errorMsg = getErrorMessage(error);
+  const contextInfo = {
+    visibilityState: typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.visibilityState : 'unknown',
+    hidden: typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.hidden : false,
+    online: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true,
+  };
+
+  if (errorMsg.includes('Auth timeout') || errorMsg.includes('Auth request was cancelled')) {
+    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
+      context: 'fetchToolSettingsSupabase.authTimeout',
+      showToast: false,
+      logData: contextInfo,
+    });
+    return toToolSettingsOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError(
+      'network',
+      'Authentication check timed out. Please retry.',
+      {
+        recoverable: true,
+        cause: error,
+        metadata: { ...contextInfo, reason: 'auth_timeout' },
+      },
+    ));
+  }
+
+  if (errorMsg.includes('Failed to fetch')) {
+    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
+      context: 'fetchToolSettingsSupabase.network',
+      showToast: false,
+      logData: contextInfo,
+    });
+    return toToolSettingsOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError(
+      'network',
+      'Network connection issue. Please check your internet connection.',
+      {
+        recoverable: true,
+        cause: error,
+        metadata: contextInfo,
+      },
+    ));
+  }
+
+  normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
+    context: 'fetchToolSettingsSupabase',
+    showToast: false,
+    logData: contextInfo,
+  });
+  return toToolSettingsOperationFailure(classifyToolSettingsError(error));
+}
diff --git a/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsScopeReader.ts b/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsScopeReader.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..fbfdfa1d2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsScopeReader.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+import { deepMerge } from '@/shared/lib/utils/deepEqual';
+import { getErrorMessage } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
+import { toolDefaultsRegistry } from '@/tooling/toolDefaultsRegistry';
+import type { ToolSettingsSupabaseClient } from './toolSettingsAuthCache';
+import { ToolSettingsError } from './toolSettingsErrors';
+
+export interface ToolSettingsContext {
+  projectId?: string;
+  shotId?: string;
+}
+
+export interface SettingsFetchResult<T = unknown> {
+  settings: T;
+  hasShotSettings: boolean;
+}
+
+type SettingsRow = { data: { settings: unknown } | null; error: unknown };
+type AbortableQuery<T> = {
+  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
+};
+
+function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
+  if (!signal) {
+    return query;
+  }
+  const abortable = query as AbortableQuery<T>;
+  if (typeof abortable.abortSignal === 'function') {
+    return abortable.abortSignal(signal);
+  }
+  return query;
+}
+
+export function fetchToolSettingsScopes(
+  supabaseClient: ToolSettingsSupabaseClient,
+  userId: string,
+  ctx: ToolSettingsContext,
+  signal?: AbortSignal,
+): Promise<[SettingsRow, SettingsRow, SettingsRow]> {
+  const userQuery = maybeAttachAbortSignal(
+    supabaseClient
+      .from('users')
+      .select('settings')
+      .eq('id', userId)
+      .maybeSingle(),
+    signal,
+  );
+  const projectQuery = ctx.projectId
+    ? maybeAttachAbortSignal(
+        supabaseClient
+          .from('projects')
+          .select('settings')
+          .eq('id', ctx.projectId)
+          .maybeSingle(),
+        signal,
+      )
+    : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null });
+  const shotQuery = ctx.shotId
+    ? maybeAttachAbortSignal(
+        supabaseClient
+          .from('shots')
+          .select('settings')
+          .eq('id', ctx.shotId)
+          .maybeSingle(),
+        signal,
+      )
+    : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null });
+
+  return Promise.all([
+    userQuery,
+    projectQuery,
+    shotQuery,
+  ]) as Promise<[SettingsRow, SettingsRow, SettingsRow]>;
+}
+
+export function mergeToolSettingsScopes(
+  userResult: SettingsRow,
+  projectResult: SettingsRow,
+  shotResult: SettingsRow,
+  toolId: string,
+  ctx: ToolSettingsContext,
+): SettingsFetchResult {
+  if (userResult.error) {
+    throw new ToolSettingsError(
+      'scope_fetch_failed',
+      `Failed to load user settings: ${getErrorMessage(userResult.error)}`,
+      { recoverable: true, cause: userResult.error, metadata: { scope: 'user' } },
+    );
+  }
+  if (ctx.projectId && projectResult.error) {
+    throw new ToolSettingsError(
+      'scope_fetch_failed',
+      `Failed to load project settings: ${getErrorMessage(projectResult.error)}`,
+      { recoverable: true, cause: projectResult.error, metadata: { scope: 'project', projectId: ctx.projectId } },
+    );
+  }
+  if (ctx.shotId && shotResult.error) {
+    throw new ToolSettingsError(
+      'scope_fetch_failed',
+      `Failed to load shot settings: ${getErrorMessage(shotResult.error)}`,
+      { recoverable: true, cause: shotResult.error, metadata: { scope: 'shot', shotId: ctx.shotId } },
+    );
+  }
+
+  const userSettingsData = userResult.data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null;
+  const projectSettingsData = projectResult.data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null;
+  const shotSettingsData = shotResult.data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null;
+  const userSettings = (userSettingsData?.[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
+  const projectSettings = (projectSettingsData?.[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
+  const shotSettings = (shotSettingsData?.[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
+  const defaultSettings = (toolDefaultsRegistry[toolId] as Record<string, unknown> | undefined) ?? {};
+  const hasShotSettings = Object.keys(shotSettings).length > 0;
+
+  return {
+    settings: deepMerge({}, defaultSettings, userSettings, projectSettings, shotSettings),
+    hasShotSettings,
+  };
+}

+/**
+ * Tool settings auth cache.
+ *
+ * Reads user ID without acquiring navigator.locks. Resolution order (all
+ * synchronous / lock-free):
+ *   1. In-memory cache (seeded during runtime bootstrap auth sync)
+ *   2. localStorage session (same key Supabase uses; contains full user object)
+ *   3. null — user is genuinely signed out
+ *
+ * Previously this called getSession() / getUser() which both acquire a shared
+ * navigator.lock. During token refresh Supabase holds an EXCLUSIVE lock, so
+ * ALL shared-lock requests queue behind it — blocking for 600ms-16s. By reading
+ * the user ID from localStorage instead we avoid locks entirely.
+ */
 import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
 import { readUserIdFromStorage } from '@/shared/lib/supabaseSession';
 import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

+
+export function getOrInitializeSupabaseRuntimeClientResult(): SupabaseClientAccessResult {
+  const existing = getSupabaseRuntimeClientResult();
+  if (existing.ok || runtimeError) {
+    return existing;
+  }
+
+  try {
+    return {
+      ok: true,
+      client: initializeSupabaseClientRuntime(),
+    };
+  } catch (error) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      error: normalizeSupabaseError(error),
+    };
+  }
+}

+import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Types & interfaces
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+type ToolSettingsErrorCode =
+  | 'auth_required'
+  | 'cancelled'
+  | 'network'
+  | 'scope_fetch_failed'
+  | 'invalid_scope_identifier'
+  | 'unknown';
+
+interface ToolSettingsErrorOptions {
+  recoverable?: boolean;
+  cause?: unknown;
+  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
+}
+
+export interface ToolSettingsSupabaseClient {
+  from: (table: string) => {
+    select: (columns: string) => {
+      eq: (column: string, value: string) => {
+        maybeSingle: () => Promise<unknown>;
+        abortSignal?: <T>(signal: AbortSignal) => T;
+      };
+    };
+  };
+  auth: {
+    getSession: () => Promise<{ data: { session: Session | null } }>;
+    onAuthStateChange?: (
+      callback: AuthStateCallback,
+    ) => {
+      data?: {
+        subscription?: {
+          unsubscribe?: () => void;
+        };
+      };
+    };
+  };
+}
+
+export interface ToolSettingsContext {
+  projectId?: string;
+  shotId?: string;
+}
+
+export interface SettingsFetchResult<T = unknown> {
+  settings: T;
+  hasShotSettings: boolean;
+}
+
+type UserLookupResult = Promise<{ data: { user: { id: string } | null }; error: null }>;
+type AuthStateCallback = (event: string, session: Session | null) => void;
+type SettingsRow = { data: { settings: unknown } | null; error: unknown };
+type AbortableQuery<T> = {
+  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
+};
+
+interface AuthCacheSyncSource {
+  subscribe: (id: string, callback: AuthStateCallback) => () => void;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Auth cache
+//
+// Reads user ID without acquiring navigator.locks. Resolution order (all
+// synchronous / lock-free):
+//   1. In-memory cache (seeded during runtime bootstrap auth sync)
+//   2. localStorage session (same key Supabase uses; contains full user object)
+//   3. null — user is genuinely signed out
+//
+// Previously this called getSession() / getUser() which both acquire a shared
+// navigator.lock. During token refresh Supabase holds an EXCLUSIVE lock, so
+// ALL shared-lock requests queue behind it — blocking for 600ms-16s. By reading
+// the user ID from localStorage instead we avoid locks entirely.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+const USER_CACHE_MS = 10_000; // 10 seconds
+
+let cachedUser: { id: string } | null = null;
+let hasCachedUserSnapshot = false;
+let cachedUserAt = 0;
+let cleanupAuthCacheSync: (() => void) | null = null;
+let authCacheInitializationPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
+let runtimeSupabaseClient: ToolSettingsSupabaseClient | null = null;
+let invalidateAuthDependentState: (() => void) | null = null;
+
+function createDirectAuthCacheSyncSource(
+  supabaseClient: ToolSettingsSupabaseClient,
+): AuthCacheSyncSource {
+  return {
+    subscribe: (_id, callback) => {
+      if (typeof supabaseClient.auth.onAuthStateChange !== 'function') {
+        return () => {};
+      }
+      const authSubscription = supabaseClient.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, session) => {
+        callback(event, session);
+      });
+      const unsubscribe = authSubscription.data?.subscription?.unsubscribe;
+      return typeof unsubscribe === 'function' ? () => unsubscribe() : () => {};
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+function updateCachedUserId(userId: string | null, invalidateState: boolean): void {
+  if (invalidateState) {
+    invalidateAuthDependentState?.();
+  }
+
+  cachedUser = userId ? { id: userId } : null;
+  hasCachedUserSnapshot = true;
+  cachedUserAt = Date.now();
+}
+
+function setToolSettingsRuntimeClient(
+  supabaseClient: ToolSettingsSupabaseClient,
+): void {
+  runtimeSupabaseClient = supabaseClient;
+}
+
+function resolveToolSettingsRuntimeClient(
+  supabaseClient?: ToolSettingsSupabaseClient,
+): ToolSettingsSupabaseClient | null {
+  return supabaseClient ?? runtimeSupabaseClient;
+}
+
+function syncCachedUserId(userId: string | null): void {
+  if (userId) {
+    setCachedUserId(userId);
+    return;
+  }
+  clearCachedUserId();
+}
+
+function syncCachedUserFromSession(session: Session | null): void {
+  syncCachedUserId(session?.user?.id ?? null);
+}

+  authManager: AuthCacheSyncSource,
